But... no amount of papers they show me changes what I’ve been seeing going on for almost two years now. \n\nYou don't just have the bank, the loan servicer, and the people at my work all acting the exact same way by coincidence. I can see that they've been quietly working together to push me out of my house. I don't need a specific piece of paper to prove it when I've watched them doing it this whole time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices that other people could not hear?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't heard any voices. It's not like that. I just see what people are doing and how they're acting. I don't hear things that aren't there.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed that strangers, coworkers, or other people were sending you special messages meant specifically for you?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't noticed anything like that. Nobody is sending me secret messages or codes. It's not some kind of puzzle I'm trying to solve. Like I said, they are just working together to force me out of my house through the foreclosure. They aren't trying to communicate with me in secret, they're just doing things behind my back. So no, no special messages.\n\nDoctor: Has the same belief been held by anyone close to you, like a family member or partner?\n\nPatient: No, not really. My family... they don't really see what's going on. They just think it's a normal foreclosure, probably because they aren't the ones dealing with these people every single day. They haven't seen all the connections firsthand like I have. So, no, it's just me.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any recent periods where you felt unusually energized or needed much less sleep than usual?\n\nPatient: No, my energy levels are pretty normal. I definitely still need my sleep. I mean, sometimes it's hard to fall asleep because I'm stressed out about the foreclosure and what they're trying to pull, but I don't have periods where I feel unusually energized or anything like that. I just sleep my normal amount whenever I can.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever seen things that other people could not see?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I don't see things that aren't there. Everything I'm seeing with this whole situation is really happening, it's not some hallucination. So, no, I've never seen things that other people couldn't see.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least a week when your mood was unusually elevated or irritable?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I mean, I definitely get irritable sometimes, but that's just regular frustration because of everything going on with the house and how they're treating me. Anyone would be mad about that. But I've never had a week where my mood was unusually elevated or anything like that. It's just normal stress.\n\nDoctor: Has anyone told you that your speech or your thoughts are hard to follow or seem disorganized?\n\nPatient: No, nobody has ever said that to me. I mean, I supervise a retail team, so I have to be pretty clear when I communicate. My thoughts are perfectly organized. Sometimes I have to explain things a few times to people who just aren't paying attention, but no... no one has ever told me my speech is disorganized or hard to follow.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a time when this belief was stronger while you were feeling very depressed?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. I mean, I'm not depressed. I'm just dealing with this situation with my house, which is incredibly stressful. But it doesn't have anything to do with my mood going up or down. I know what's happening because it's happening to me every day, not because I'm feeling sad or depressed.\n\nDoctor: Aside from this foreclosure belief, have you had any other beliefs that others would say are unusual or untrue?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. Everything else in my life is completely normal. It's just this one thing with the bank and the house. I don't have any other unusual beliefs, because what's happening to me isn't a belief—it's just a fact. But no, nothing else.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. I don't need a specific piece of paper to prove it when I've watched them doing it this whole time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices that other people could not hear?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't heard any voices. It's not like that. I just see what people are doing and how they're acting. I don't hear things that aren't there.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed that strangers, coworkers, or other people were sending you special messages meant specifically for you?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't noticed anything like that. Nobody is sending me secret messages or codes. It's not some kind of puzzle I'm trying to solve. Like I said, they are just working together to force me out of my house through the foreclosure. They aren't trying to communicate with me in secret, they're just doing things behind my back. So no, no special messages.\n\nDoctor: Has the same belief been held by anyone close to you, like a family member or partner?\n\nPatient: No, not really. My family... they don't really see what's going on. They just think it's a normal foreclosure, probably because they aren't the ones dealing with these people every single day. They haven't seen all the connections firsthand like I have. So, no, it's just me.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any recent periods where you felt unusually energized or needed much less sleep than usual?\n\nPatient: No, my energy levels are pretty normal. I definitely still need my sleep. I mean, sometimes it's hard to fall asleep because I'm stressed out about the foreclosure and what they're trying to pull, but I don't have periods where I feel unusually energized or anything like that. I just sleep my normal amount whenever I can.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever seen things that other people could not see?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I don't see things that aren't there. Everything I'm seeing with this whole situation is really happening, it's not some hallucination. So, no, I've never seen things that other people couldn't see.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least a week when your mood was unusually elevated or irritable?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I mean, I definitely get irritable sometimes, but that's just regular frustration because of everything going on with the house and how they're treating me. Anyone would be mad about that. But I've never had a week where my mood was unusually elevated or anything like that. It's just normal stress.\n\nDoctor: Has anyone told you that your speech or your thoughts are hard to follow or seem disorganized?\n\nPatient: No, nobody has ever said that to me. I mean, I supervise a retail team, so I have to be pretty clear when I communicate. My thoughts are perfectly organized. Sometimes I have to explain things a few times to people who just aren't paying attention, but no... no one has ever told me my speech is disorganized or hard to follow.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a time when this belief was stronger while you were feeling very depressed?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. I mean, I'm not depressed. I'm just dealing with this situation with my house, which is incredibly stressful. But it doesn't have anything to do with my mood going up or down. I know what's happening because it's happening to me every day, not because I'm feeling sad or depressed.\n\nDoctor: Aside from this foreclosure belief, have you had any other beliefs that others would say are unusual or untrue?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. Everything else in my life is completely normal. It's just this one thing with the bank and the house. I don't have any other unusual beliefs, because what's happening to me isn't a belief—it's just a fact. But no, nothing else.\n\nDoctor: Has anyone close to you, like a family member or someone you live with, come to believe this same thing about the foreclosure and people working together against you?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I live alone, so there isn't anyone here seeing it happen day to day. And when I try to explain it to my family... they just don't get it. They think I'm just stressed about money or misreading the letters. They definitely don't see that it's being done on purpose. So, no, it's just me dealing with it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever used any recreational drugs, including stimulants like cocaine or methamphetamine, around the time this belief started?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I don't use drugs. I've never touched anything like that, not cocaine, not meth, nothing. I barely even drink. This isn't about drugs, it's just about what the bank is doing to me.\n\nDoctor: Since this started, has it affected your work, relationships, or ability to function day to day?\n\nPatient: Well, I'm still working. I haven't lost my job or anything. But... things have definitely gotten harder. My marriage is pretty tense right now. Every time I try to talk about the foreclosure, it just turns into an argument, so we're fighting a lot more. And at work, I've had a few run-ins with my managers over just... regular paperwork. Nothing major, but I've noticed people are acting a little more careful around me lately. So, yeah, it's affecting things, but I'm still functioning.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. If it did not change, leave \"note\" empty.\n- If the candidates have narrowed to one and you are confident enough that no further questioning is needed, return true for is_final.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"candidates\": [\"<ICD-10 code>\", \"...\"], \"note\": \"<why the list changed, or empty>\", \"is_final\": <true or false>}\n"

So, yeah, it's affecting things, but I'm still functioning.\n\nDoctor: How long have you held this belief about the foreclosure and the bank working together against you?\n\nPatient: It's been... about a year and nine months now.
